# Dankornas radar: merged triage of all 2,105 promoted repos (Jan-Nov 2026)

Merged from the five shard triages (`dankornas-shard-{1..5}-of-5.md`, repos 1-2105 of
`library/analysis/dankornas-promotions-2026-full.json`). The shard ranges were verified
disjoint at merge time: 2105 unique repos, zero cross-shard duplicates.
First-promotion dates span 2026-01-01 to 2026-11-05.
